### Verilating Verilog sources...
verilator -Wno-fatal -Wall --top-module tb_accelerator \
		-cc --timing -O3 --x-assign fast --x-initial fast --noassert --Mdir obj_dir \
		--threads 8 \
		tb_accelerator.v accelerator.v loader.v writer.v data_formatter.v sa_enhanced.v pe.v sram_banked.v sram_c.v  --exe sim_main.cpp
